&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\operatorname{Tr} \left(Q_{n(T-1)}^\top \left(\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A^\top\right)\right)\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)}\right)\right)^{1/2} \\
&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\operatorname{Tr} \left(Q_{n(T-1)}^\top \left(G \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes (A^\top A)\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)}\right)\right)^{1/2} \\
&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\operatorname{Tr} \left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\right)^{1/2} \\
&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\operatorname{Tr} \left(G_t \odot A^\top A\right)\right)^{1/2} \\
&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \sum_{i=1}^n (G_t)_{ii} (A^\top A)_{ii}\right)^{1/2} \\
& \le \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \max_{i} \ensuremath{\left | (G_t)_{ii} \right |}\sum_{i=1}^n \ensuremath{\left | (A^\top A)_{ii}  \right |}\right)^{1/2}  \\
&\le \left(\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} J^2(A^*)\right)^{1/2} \le J(A^*) \sqrt{T}.
\end{align*}

Let $t = V_3 \sqrt{u}$ for $u \ge 1$. Since $V_3 \ge U_3$ this means,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\sup_{M_3 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3} \ensuremath{\left | \big\|{M_3\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \big\|{M_3 \widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2  \right |} \ge c_1F_3+V_3\sqrt{u}\right) \le 2\exp (-c_2\sqrt{u}).
\]
 
Note that,
 
\begin{align*}
F_3 &= \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+d_{F}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3)\right)+d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3)d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3)\\
&\le \bar{C}_2J(A^*) \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \left(\bar{C}_2J(A^*) \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+J(A^*) \sqrt{T}\right)+J^2(A^*) \sqrt{T}\\
&\le \bar{C}_2^2 J^2(A^*) \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}^2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) +\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \sqrt{T}+\sqrt{T}\right)\\
&=\bar{F}_1,
\end{align*}
 
and,
 
\begin{align*}
V_3 &= d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+d_{F}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3)\right)\\
&\le J(A^*) \left(\bar{C}_2J(A^*)\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) +J(A^*)\sqrt{T}\right)\\
&\le \bar{C}_2 J^2(A^*)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) +\sqrt{T}\right)\\
&=\bar{V}_1.
\end{align*}
 
Then,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\sup_{M_3 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3} \ensuremath{\left | \big\|{M_3\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \big\|{M_3\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2  \right |} \ge c_1\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\sqrt{u}\right) \le 2\exp (-c_2\sqrt{u}).
\]

Letting $u = c\log^2 (2/\delta)$ (for sufficiently large $c$) we have with probability at least $1-\delta$, that 
$$\forall M_3 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3: \  \big\|{M_3\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2\in \left(\big\|{M_3}\big\|_F^2 \pm \bar{c}_2 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1 \log (1/\delta)\right)\right),$$
 
which completes the proof.
<!-- end proof-14 -->
<!-- remark-13: body_md -->
\label{rem:thm-had-kron}
Let $A,B \in \ensuremath{\mathbb{R}}^{n \times m}$. Then, 
\[
A \odot B = Q_n^\top \bigg(A \otimes B\bigg) Q_m
\]
where $Q_n^\top = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \left(e_1^{(n)}{e_1^{(n)}}^\top\right) & \left(e_2^{(n)}{e_2^{(n)}}^\top\right) & \dots & \left(e_n^{(n)}{e_n^{(n)}}^\top\right) \end{bmatrix}}$ ($e_i^{(n)}$ is the $i$-th standard basis vector in $\ensuremath{\mathbb{R}}^n$).
<!-- end remark-13 -->
<!-- claim-4: body_md -->
\label{clm:lip-map-M3}
The map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2 \right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right)$ is $J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end claim-4 -->
<!-- proof-15: body_md -->
Consider $A_1, A_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$ and the corresponding $\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_1\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)} \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3$ and $\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_2\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)} \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3$. Note that,
 
\begin{align*}
& \big\|{\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_1\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)}-\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_2\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)}}\big\|_2\\ 
& \le \big\|{\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_1\right)\right)-\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_2\right)\right)}\big\|_2\\
& = \big\|{\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes (A_1-A_2)\right)\right)}\big\|_2 \le \big\|{G}\big\|_2^{1/2} \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2.
\end{align*}
 
Since $\big\|{G}\big\|_2 = \max_{t} \big\|{G_t}\big\|_2 \le \big\|{\Gamma \Gamma^\top}\big\|_2 \le J^2(A^*)$, we get,
 
\begin{align*}
\big\|{\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_1\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)}-\left(G^{1/2} \otimes \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A_2\right)\right) Q_{n(T-1)}}\big\|_2 \le J(A^*) \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2.
\end{align*}
 
Therefore, the map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2 \right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right)$ is $J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end proof-15 -->
<!-- proof-16: body_md -->
Let $\widetilde{q} = \frac{1}{\sqrt{p(1-p)}}q$. It is easy to see that the variance of the entries of $\widetilde{q}$ is $1$. Let 
\begin{align*}
\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4 &= \left\{{\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}: A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}. 
\end{align*}

Applying the Theorem~\ref{thm:psd-sup} gives us
 
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\sup_{M_4 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4} \ensuremath{\left | \big\|{M_4\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \big\|{M_4\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2 \right |} \ge c_1F_4+t\right) \le 2\exp \left(-c_2 \min \left\{{\frac{t^2}{V_4},\frac{t}{U_4}}\right\}\right),
\]
 
where,
 
\begin{align*}
F_4 &= \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+d_{F}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)\right)+d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4),\\
V_4 &= d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+d_{F}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)\right),\; \text{and}, \; U_4 = d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)^2.
\end{align*}

Proceeding as in the proof of Claim \ref{clm:lip-map}, we obtain the following claim.
 
 
                                       
                                       
                                       
\begin{claim}\label{clm:lip-map-M4}
The map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2 \right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right)$ is $J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
\end{claim}
 
 
Since the map from $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2 \right)$ to $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right)$ is Lipschitz and onto, Property \ref{itm:prop-3} from Appendix~\ref{sec:prelim-tools} implies,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \le CJ(A^*) \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2),
\]
 
for some constant $C \ge 1$. Using the definitions of $d_2(\cdot), d_F(\cdot)$ from Section~\ref{sec:prelim-tools} gives us,
 
\begin{align*}
d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4) &= \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_2 \\
&= \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{\left(\operatorname{diag}(G) \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_2 \\
&= \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(G)  \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)}\big\|_2 \\
& \le \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(G)}\big\|_2 \big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(A^\top A)}\big\|\\
&=\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\big\|{\operatorname{diag}(G)}\big\|_2^{1/2} \big\|{\operatorname{diag}(A^\top A)}\big\|^{1/2} \le \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\big\|{G}\big\|_2^{1/2} \big\|{A^\top A}\big\|_2^{1/2} \\
&\le \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}J(A^*) \big\|{A}\big\|_2 \le J(A^*) 
\end{align*}
 
and,
 
\begin{align*}
d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4) &= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_F^2\right)^{1/2} \\
&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\operatorname{Tr} \left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\right)^{1/2} \\
&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1}\operatorname{Tr} \left(G_t \odot \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)^{1/2} \\
&= \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \sum_{i=1}^n (G_t)_{ii} (A^\top A)_{ii}\right)^{1/2} \\
& \le \left(\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \max_{i} \ensuremath{\left | (G_t)_{ii} \right |}\sum_{i=1}^n \ensuremath{\left | (A^\top A)_{ii}  \right |}\right)^{1/2}  \\
&\le \left(\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} J^2(A^*)\right)^{1/2} \le J(A^*) \sqrt{T}.
\end{align*}

Let $t = V_4 \sqrt{u}$ for $u \ge 1$. Since $V_4 \ge U_4$ this means,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\sup_{M_4 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4} \ensuremath{\left | \big\|{M_4\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \big\|{M_4 \widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2  \right |} \ge c_1F_4+V_4\sqrt{u}\right) \le 2\exp (-c_2\sqrt{u}).
\]
 
Note that,
 
\begin{align*}
F_4 &= \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+d_{F}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)\right)+d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)\\
&\le \bar{C}_2J(A^*) \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \left(\bar{C}_2 J(A^*) \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+J(A^*) \sqrt{T}\right)+J^2(A^*) \sqrt{T}\\
&\le \bar{C}_2^2 J^2(A^*) \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}^2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) +\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \sqrt{T}+\sqrt{T}\right)\\
&=\bar{F}_1,
\end{align*}
 
and,
 
\begin{align*}
V_4 &= d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+d_{F}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4)\right)\\
&\le J(A^*) \left(\bar{C}_2 J(A^*)\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) +J(A^*)\sqrt{T}\right)\\
&\le \bar{C}_2 J^2(A^*)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) +\sqrt{T}\right)\\
&=\bar{V}_1.
\end{align*}
 
Then we have,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\sup_{M_4 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4} \ensuremath{\left | \big\|{M_4\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \big\|{M_4\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2  \right |} \ge c_1\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\sqrt{u}\right) \le 2\exp (-c_2\sqrt{u}).
\]
 
Letting $u = c\log^2 (2/\delta)$ (for sufficiently large $c$) we have with probability at least $1-\delta$, that 
 
$$\forall M_4 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4: \ \big\|{M_4\widetilde{q}}\big\|_2^2\le \big\|{M_4}\big\|_F^2+\bar{c}_2 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1 \log (1/\delta)\right).$$ 
 
This completes the proof.
<!-- end proof-16 -->
<!-- claim-5: body_md -->
\label{clm:lip-map-M4}
The map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2 \right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right)$ is $J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end claim-5 -->
<!-- proof-17: body_md -->
Consider $A_1, A_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$ and the corresponding $\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}(A_1^\top A_1)\right)\right)^{1/2} \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4$ and $\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}(A_2^\top A_2)\right)\right)^{1/2} \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_4$. Note that,
 
\begin{align*}
&\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}-\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}\\
&=\left(\operatorname{diag}(G) \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}-\left(\operatorname{diag}(G) \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}\\
&=\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(G)\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)-\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)\right)\right).
\end{align*}
 
Therefore,
 
\begin{align*}
& \big\|{\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}-\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_2\\ 
& \le \big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(G)\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)-\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)\right)\right)}\big\|_2\\
&\le \big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(G)}\big\|_2 \big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)-\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)}\big\|_2.
\end{align*}
 
Note that,
\begin{align*}
\big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(G)}\big\|_2 &= \max_i \sqrt{G_{ii}} = \left(\max_i G_{ii}\right)^{1/2}\\
&= \left(\max_i \frac{e_i^\top G e_i}{e_i^\top e_i}\right)^{1/2} \; \text{(Where $e_i$ is the $i$-th standard basis vector)}\\
& \le \big\|{G}\big\|_2^{1/2} \le J(A^*)
\end{align*}
and,
\begin{align*}
&\big\|{\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)-\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)}\big\|_2 \\
&= \big\|{\operatorname{diag}\left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \big\|{(A_1)_{:,1}}\big\|_2 & \big\|{(A_1)_{:,2}}\big\|_2 & \dots & \big\|{(A_1)_{:,n}}\big\|_2 \end{bmatrix}}\right)-\operatorname{diag}\left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \big\|{(A_2)_{:,1}}\big\|_2 & \big\|{(A_2)_{:,2}}\big\|_2 & \dots & \big\|{(A_2)_{:,n}}\big\|_2 \end{bmatrix}}\right)}\big\|_2\\
&=\max_{i} \ensuremath{\left | \big\|{(A_1)_{:,i}}\big\|_2-\big\|{(A_2)_{:,i}}\big\|_2 \right |} \le \max_i \big\|{(A_1)_{:,i}-(A_1)_{:,i}}\big\|_2 \le \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2
\end{align*}
where,  $(A_1)_{:,i}, (A_2)_{:,i}$ are the $i$-th columns of $A_1, A_2$. Therefore,
 
\begin{align*}
\big\|{\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_1^\top A_1\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}-\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A_2^\top A_2\right)\right)\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_2 \le J(A^*) \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2.
\end{align*}
 
and therefore, the map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2 \right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{M}}_3,\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right)$ is $J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end proof-17 -->
<!-- proof-18: body_md -->
Consider $2p\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle$. Note that since $\overline{P}$ is a diagonal matrix, 
 
\[
2p\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle = 2p\Big\langle\overline{P},\operatorname{diag} \left(\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\right)\Big\rangle.
\]
 
Given $G_1,G_2,\dots,G_{T-1}$, 
 
\begin{equation*}
2p\Big\langle\overline{P},\operatorname{diag} \left(\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\right)\Big\rangle =2p\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleP_t,\operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A G_t\right)\Big\rangle =2p\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleq_t,r_t(A)\Big\rangle,
\end{equation*}
 
where $r_t(A)= \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A G_t\right)_{11} & \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A G_t\right)_{22} & \dots & \operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A G_t\right)_{dd} \end{bmatrix}}^\top$ for all $t \in [T-1]$. Denoting 
$$r(A) = \operatorname{vec} \left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} r_1(A) & r_2(A) & \dots & r_{T-1}(A) \end{bmatrix}}\right),$$ we get,
 
\begin{equation*}
2p\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle =2p\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleq_t,r_t(A)\Big\rangle =2p \Big\langleq,r(A)\Big\rangle.
\end{equation*}

Now consider $2p(1-p)\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(\operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle$. Note that,
 
\[
2p(1-p)\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(\operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle = 2p(1-p)\Big\langle\overline{P},\operatorname{diag} \left(\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(\operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\right)\Big\rangle,
\]
 
which gives, 
 
\begin{align*}
2p(1-p)\Big\langle\overline{P},\operatorname{diag} \left(\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(\operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\right)\Big\rangle &=2p(1-p)\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleP_t,\operatorname{diag} \left(\operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right) G_t\right)\Big\rangle\\
&=2p(1-p)\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleq_t,s_t(A)\Big\rangle,
\end{align*}
 
where $s_t(A)= \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \operatorname{diag} \left(\operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right) G_t\right)_{11} & \dots & \operatorname{diag} \left(\operatorname{diag} \left(A^\top A\right) G_t\right)_{dd} \end{bmatrix}}^\top$ for all $t \in [T-1]$. Letting $s(A) = \operatorname{vec} \left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} s_1(A) & s_2(A) & \dots & s_{T-1}(A) \end{bmatrix}}\right)$, we get,
 
\begin{align*}
2p(1-p)\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(\operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle &=2p(1-p)\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleq_t,s_t(A)\Big\rangle\\
&=2p(1-p) \Big\langleq,s(A)\Big\rangle.
\end{align*}
 
Let $\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}} =\left\{{r(A): A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$ and $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}} =\left\{{s(A): A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. Then,
 
\begin{align*}
2p\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A-(1-p)\operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle &= 2p \Big\langleq,r(A)\Big\rangle-2p(1-p)\Big\langleq,s(A)\Big\rangle\\
&\ge -2p\sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle-2p(1-p)\sup_{s\in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \Big\langleq,s\Big\rangle.
\end{align*}

We can now upper bound $\sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle$. Note that $-q \in \ensuremath{\mathbb{R}}^{n(T-1)}$ has i.i.d, centered and bounded entries in $[-1,1]$ and therefore $1$-subgaussian. Note that $g_1(x) = \sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langlex,r\Big\rangle$ is convex and $J(A^*)\sqrt{T}$-Lipschitz (w.r.t $\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2$). Convexity is a direct result of linearity and Lipschitz bounds come from that fact that $g_1(x)-g_1(y) \le \big\|{x-y}\big\|_2 \sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \big\|{r}\big\|_2$ (since for any $r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}$, $\big\|{r}\big\|_2^2 \le J^2(A^*)T$). 

In order to bound $\sup_{r\in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle$, we use the following remark from \cite{HDPbook}:
 
 
\begin{remark}[Remark 8.5.9 of \cite{HDPbook}]\label{rem:gaus-width} 
If $X$ is a $0$-mean subgaussian vector with $\big\|{X}\big\|_{\psi_2}=K$, then, $\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \sup_{t\in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \Big\langleX,t\Big\rangle \le c K w(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}})$ for any bounded set $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}} \subset \ensuremath{\mathbb{R}}^{n}$.
\end{remark}
 
 

Applying Remark~\ref{rem:gaus-width} on $\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}$ implies $\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle \le cw(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}})$ and from Talagrand's majorizing measure theorem~\cite[Theorem 2.4.1]{talagrand2014upper}, 
 
\[
w(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}) \asymp \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right) = \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right),
\]
 
where $\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat} = \left\{{\operatorname{blkdiag}\left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \operatorname{diag}\left((A^\top A) G_1 \right) & \dots & \operatorname{diag}\left((A^\top A) G_{T-1} \right) \end{bmatrix}}\right):A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. 

 
 
                                                        
                                                        
                                                        
\begin{claim}\label{clm:lip-map-Rmat}
The map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is $2\sqrt{T} J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
\end{claim}
 
 
Since the map from $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ to $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is Lipschitz and onto (it is easy to see this simply by definition), Property \ref{itm:prop-3} from Appendix~\ref{sec:prelim-tools} implies,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \le C\sqrt{T}J(A^*)\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right).
\]
 
Furthermore, 
 
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\ensuremath{\left |  \sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle  \right |} \ge t\right) \le 2\exp \left(-\frac{t^2}{8TJ^2(A^*)}\right).
\]
 
Note that $$\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \sup_{r \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle = w(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}) = \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \le C\sqrt{T}J(A^*)\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right).$$ Set $t = C\sqrt{T}J(A^*) \sqrt{\log (2/\delta)}$ and denote
 
\[
\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1c}} = \left\{{\sup_{r\in R} \Big\langle-q,r\Big\rangle \le C\sqrt{T}J(A^*)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (2/\delta)}\right)}\right\}.
\]
 
Then,
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1c}}\right) \ge 1-\delta.
\]
We can see that by following the same argument, we have with probability at least $1-\delta$, $$\sup_{r\in R} \Big\langleq,r\Big\rangle \le C\sqrt{T}J(A^*)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (2/\delta)}\right).$$
 
 
Therefore, with probability at least $1-\delta$, 
\[
\Big\langle\overline{P},\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma \Gamma^\top\Big\rangle \le C\sqrt{T}J(A^*)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right).
\]

Next we can upper bound $\sup_{s \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \Big\langleq,s\Big\rangle$. Note that $q \in \ensuremath{\mathbb{R}}^{n(T-1)}$ has i.i.d, centered and bounded entries in $[-1,1]$ and therefore $1$-subgaussian. We can see that $g_2(x) = \sup_{s \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \Big\langlex,s\Big\rangle$ is convex and also $J(A^*)\sqrt{T}$-Lipschitz. This can be verified following the same arguments as for $g_1(x)$. 

Applying Remark~\ref{rem:gaus-width} on $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}$ implies $\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \sup_{s \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \Big\langleq,s\Big\rangle \le c w(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}})$, hence from Talagrand's majorizing measure theorem~\cite[Theorem 2.4.1]{talagrand2014upper}, we obtain
 
\[
w(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}) \asymp \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right) = \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right),
\]
 
where $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat} = \left\{{\operatorname{blkdiag}\left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \operatorname{diag}\left(\operatorname{diag}(A^\top A) G_1 \right) & \dots & \operatorname{diag}\left(\operatorname{diag}(A^\top A) G_{T-1} \right) \end{bmatrix}}\right):A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. 
 
 
                                                        
                                                        
                                                        
\begin{claim}\label{clm:lip-map-Smat}
The map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is $2\sqrt{T} J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
\end{claim}
 
 
Since the map from $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ to $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is Lipschitz and onto (it is easy to see this simply by definition), Property \ref{itm:prop-3} from Appendix~\ref{sec:prelim-tools} implies,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \le C\sqrt{T}J(A^*)\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right).
\]
 
Denote the event
 
\[
\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2c}} = \left\{{\sup_{s \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \Big\langleq,s\Big\rangle \le C\sqrt{T}J(A^*)\left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (2/\delta)}\right)}\right\}.
\]
 
Then similar to before,
 
\[
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2c}}\right) \ge 1-\delta.
\]

Putting events $\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1c}}, \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2c}}$ together, we get that w.p. at least $1-2\delta$, it holds for all $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$,
 
\[
2p \Big\langleq,r(A)\Big\rangle-2p(1-p)\Big\langleq,s(A)\Big\rangle \ge -cpJ(A^*)\sqrt{T} \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right),
\]
 
for a suitably large constant $c$.
<!-- end proof-18 -->
<!-- remark-14: body_md -->
\label{rem:gaus-width} 
If $X$ is a $0$-mean subgaussian vector with $\big\|{X}\big\|_{\psi_2}=K$, then, $\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \sup_{t\in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \Big\langleX,t\Big\rangle \le c K w(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}})$ for any bounded set $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}} \subset \ensuremath{\mathbb{R}}^{n}$.
<!-- end remark-14 -->
<!-- claim-6: body_md -->
\label{clm:lip-map-Rmat}
The map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is $2\sqrt{T} J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end claim-6 -->
<!-- claim-7: body_md -->
\label{clm:lip-map-Smat}
The map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is $2\sqrt{T} J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end claim-7 -->
<!-- proof-19: body_md -->
Consider $A_1, A_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$ and the corresponding $R_1, R_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat}$. Note that,
 
\begin{align*}
\big\|{R_1-R_2}\big\|_F^2 &= \sum_{t=1}^T \big\|{\operatorname{diag} \left(A_1^\top A_1 G_t\right)-\operatorname{diag} \left(A_2^\top A_2 G_t\right)}\big\|_F^2\\
&\le \sum_{t=1}^T \big\|{A_1^\top A_1 G_t-A_2^\top A_2 G_t}\big\|_F^2\\
&\le J^2(A^*) \sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \big\|{A_1^\top A_1-A_2^\top A_2}\big\|_F^2 \\
&\le 4J^2(A^*) T \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F^2.
\end{align*}
 
Therefore, the map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is $2\sqrt{T} J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end proof-19 -->
<!-- proof-20: body_md -->
Consider $A_1, A_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$ and the corresponding $S_1, S_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat}$. Note that,
 
\begin{align*}
\big\|{S_1-S_2}\big\|_F^2 &= \sum_{t=1}^T \big\|{\operatorname{diag} \left(\operatorname{diag}(A_1^\top A_1) G_t\right)-\operatorname{diag} \left(\operatorname{diag}(A_2^\top A_2) G_t\right)}\big\|_F^2\\
&\le \sum_{t=1}^T \big\|{\operatorname{diag}(A_1^\top A_1) G_t-\operatorname{diag}(A_2^\top A_2) G_t}\big\|_F^2\\
&\le J^2(A^*) \sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \big\|{A_1^\top A_1-A_2^\top A_2}\big\|_F^2 \\
&\le 4J^2(A^*) T \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F^2.
\end{align*}
 
Therefore, the map $\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \mapsto \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}_{mat},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)$ is $2\sqrt{T} J(A^*)$-Lipschitz.
<!-- end proof-20 -->
<!-- proof-21: body_md -->
In order to derive the bounds in Lemma~\ref{lem:RHS-bound}, we need to bound the following three terms.
 
 
\begin{enumerate}
\item $2 \Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,(\widehat{A}-B)Z\Big\rangle+2(1-p)\Big\langleA^*\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle$ \label{itm:cross-prod-lem8},
\item $2\big\|{(A^*-B)Z}\big\|_F\big\|{(\widehat{A}-B)Z}\big\|_F$,
\item $2(1-p)\big\|{(A^*-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F\big\|{(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F$.
\end{enumerate}

Let $\bar{A} = \frac{\widehat{A}-B}{\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F}$ and $\widetilde{A}^* = \frac{A^*-B}{\big\|{A^*-B}\big\|_F}$. Since $\widehat{A},A^* \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{K}}$, we have $\bar{A},\widetilde{A}^* \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$. Therefore
\begin{align*}
2\big\|{(A^*-B)Z}\big\|_F\big\|{(\widehat{A}-B)Z}\big\|_F &= 2 \big\|{A^*-B}\big\|_F\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \big\|{\widetilde{A}^* Z}\big\|_F\big\|{\bar{A} Z}\big\|_F\\ 
&\le 2 \big\|{A^*-B}\big\|_F\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{AZ}\big\|_F \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{AZ}\big\|_F \\
&= 2 \big\|{A^*-B}\big\|_F\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{A Z}\big\|_F^2.
\end{align*}
Similarly,
\begin{align*}
&2(1-p)\big\|{(A^*-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F\big\|{(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F \\
&= 2(1-p) \big\|{A^*-B}\big\|_F\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \big\|{\widetilde{A}^*\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F\big\|{\bar{A}\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F\\ 
&\leq 2(1-p) \big\|{A^*-B}\big\|_F\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{A\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F^2.
\end{align*}
Recall Lemmas~\ref{lem:M1-expec} and ~\ref{lem:M2-expec} from the proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:LHS-bound} and the definitions of $\breve{P}$ and $\overline{P}$.                                  
By Lemmas~\ref{lem:M1-expec} and~\ref{lem:M2-expec}, we have for any $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, on the event $\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1a}}\cap \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2a}}$,
\begin{align*}
&\big\|{AZ}\big\|_F^2 \le \big\|{(I_{T-1} \otimes A) \breve{P} \Gamma}\big\|_F^2+\bar{c}_1\left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right),\; \text{and},\\
&(1-p)\big\|{A\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F^2 \le (1-p) \big\|{(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(A^\top A)) \breve{P} \Gamma}\big\|_F^2+\bar{c}_1(1-p)\left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right),
\end{align*}
where $\bar{F}_1, \bar{V}_1$ are as in Lemmas ~\ref{lem:M1-expec} and ~\ref{lem:M2-expec}. Following the same arguments as in the proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:LHS-bound}, 
 
\begin{align*}
\big\|{AZ}\big\|_{F}^2 &\le \big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\overline{P}\Gamma}\big\|_F^2+2p\Big\langle\left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\overline{P}\Gamma,\left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\Gamma\Big\rangle \\
&+ p^2 \big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\Gamma}\big\|_F^2+\bar{c}_1 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right),
\end{align*}
\quad \text{and} \quad
\begin{align*}
&(1-p)\big\|{A \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_{F}^2\\
&\le (1-p)\big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\overline{P}\Gamma}\big\|_F^2+p^2(1-p)\big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma}\big\|_F^2\\
&+2p(1-p)\Big\langle\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\overline{P}\Gamma,\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma\Big\rangle+\bar{c}_1 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right).
\end{align*}
Using Lemmas ~\ref{lem:M3-expec}, ~\ref{lem:M4-expec}, and ~\ref{lem:SR-expec}, under the event $\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1b}}\cap \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2b}} \cap \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2c}} \cap \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{3c}}$ (see proofs of Lemmas~\ref{lem:LHS-bound} and~\ref{lem:SR-expec}), we have for all $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, 
\begin{align*}
\big\|{AZ}\big\|_{F}^2 &\le p(1-p)\big\|{\left(G \odot (I_{T-1} \otimes (A^\top A))\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_F^2+ p^2 \big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\Gamma}\big\|_F^2\\
&+c_3 pJ(A^*)\sqrt{T} \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right) \\
&+\bar{c}_1 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right)+p(1-p) \bar{c}_2 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right),
\end{align*}
and,
\begin{align*}
&(1-p)\big\|{A \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_{F}^2\\
&\le p(1-p)^2 \big\|{\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}(A^\top A)\right)\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_F^2+p^2(1-p)\big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma}\big\|_F^2\\
&+c_3 p(1-p)J(A^*)\sqrt{T} \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right)\\
&+\bar{c}_1 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right) +p(1-p)^2 \bar{c}_2\left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right),
\end{align*}
where $\bar{c}_1,\bar{c}_2,$ and, $c_3$ are  constants.

A simple calculation shows that
\begin{align*}
&\big\|{\left(G \odot (I_{T-1} \otimes (A^\top A))\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_F^2 \le J^2(A^*) T \; \tag{see $d_F(.)$ bounds in Lemma~\ref{lem:M3-expec}}, \\
&\big\|{\left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}(A^\top A)\right)\right)^{1/2}}\big\|_F^2 \le J^2(A^*) T \; \tag{see $d_F(.)$ bounds in Lemma~\ref{lem:M4-expec}},\\ &\big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\Gamma}\big\|_F^2 \le J^2(A^*) T, \quad \text{and}, \\
&\big\|{\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}\left(A^\top A\right)\right)\Gamma}\big\|_F^2 \le J^2(A^*)T.
\end{align*}
Therefore, we have
\begin{align}\label{eq:norm1-unif-bound-PGD}
\big\|{AZ}\big\|_{F}^2 &\le pJ^2(A^*)T+\bar{c}_1 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right)+p(1-p) \bar{c}_2 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right)\\
&+c_3 pJ(A^*)\sqrt{T} \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right),
\end{align}
and
\begin{align}\label{eq:norm2-unif-bound-PGD}
&(1-p)\big\|{A \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_{F}^2\\
&\le p(1-p) J^2(A^*) T+\bar{c}_1 \left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right) +p(1-p)^2 \bar{c}_2\left(\bar{F}_1+\bar{V}_1\log (1/\delta)\right)\\
&+c_3 p(1-p)J(A^*)\sqrt{T} \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right).
\end{align}

It remains to bound $2 \Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,(\widehat{A}-B)Z\Big\rangle+2(1-p)\Big\langleA^*\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle$. To do this, we first show that it can be written as quadratics of centered 1-subgaussian random variables, as seen in Claims~\ref{clm:quad-dotprod-direct} and ~\ref{clm:quad-dotprod-diag}. 

\begin{claim}\label{clm:quad-dotprod-direct} Let $\bar{\eta} = \operatorname{vec}(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \eta_1 & \eta_2 & \dots & \eta_T \end{bmatrix}})$, and 
\[
\breve{P} = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} P_1 & 0 & \dots & 0\\ 0 & P_2 & \dots & 0\\\vdots & \vdots & \dots & \vdots\\ 0 & 0 & \dots & P_{T-1} \end{bmatrix}}, 
 
 
\widetilde{P} = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} P_2 & 0 & \dots & 0\\ 0 & P_3 & \dots & 0\\\vdots & \vdots & \dots & \vdots\\ 0 & 0 & \dots & P_{T} \end{bmatrix}}, \text{ and } \bar{A} = \frac{\widehat{A}-B}{\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F}.
\] Then, it holds that
 
\begin{align*}
\Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)Z\Big\rangle & \\
= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\bar{\eta}^\top \bigg(&   \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma & 0\\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}}\\
&+\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0 & 0\\
\widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \end{bmatrix}}\bigg) \bar{\eta}.
\end{align*}
\end{claim}

\begin{claim}\label{clm:quad-dotprod-diag}
Let 
\begin{equation*}
    \breve{P} = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} P_1 & 0 & \dots & 0\\ 0 & P_2 & \dots & 0\\\vdots & \vdots & \dots & \vdots\\ 0 & 0 & \dots & P_{T-1} \end{bmatrix}}, \quad \text{and } \bar{A} = \frac{\widehat{A}-B}{\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F}.
\end{equation*}
Then, it holds that
 
\begin{equation*}
\Big\langleA^* \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F 
\bar{\eta}^\top \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(\bar{A}^\top A^*\right)\right) \Gamma & 0 \\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}} \bar{\eta},
\end{equation*}
\end{claim}
The proofs of Claims~\ref{clm:quad-dotprod-direct} and ~\ref{clm:quad-dotprod-diag} are deferred to Appendixes~\ref{appsec:proof-clm-quad-dotprod-direct} and ~\ref{appsec:proof-clm-quad-dotprod-diag}.
 
We are now ready to bound the inner product terms with Claims~\ref{clm:quad-dotprod-direct} and ~\ref{clm:quad-dotprod-diag}. For any $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, let 

\begin{align*}
R_1(A) &= \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma & 0\\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}},\\
R_2(A) &= \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0 & 0\\
\widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes A) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \end{bmatrix}},\text{ and}\\
R_3(A) &=(1-p) \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right) \Gamma & 0 \\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}}.
\end{align*} 
Then, we have
\begin{align*}
&\Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,AZ\Big\rangle+(1-p)\Big\langleA^* \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),A\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle\\
&=\bar{\eta}^\top \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma & 0\\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}}\bar{\eta}\\
&+\eta^\top \bigg( \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0 & 0\\
\widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes A) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \end{bmatrix}}+(1-p)\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right) \Gamma & 0 \\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}}\bigg) \bar{\eta}\\
&=\bar{\eta}^\top \bigg(R_1(A)+R_2(A)+R_3(A)\bigg) \bar{\eta},
\end{align*}
where $R_1(A), R_2(A)$, and $R_3(A)$ are random matrices depending on the random variables $(P_t)_{t=1}^T$. Conditional on $(P_t)_{t=1}^T$, let 
$$\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}} = \left\{{R_1(A)+R_2(A)+R_3(A): \  A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}.$$ 
Then, we can show the following.
 
 
\begin{lemma}\label{lem:dotprod-fixedP}
Let $\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}} = \left\{{R_1(A)+R_2(A)+R_3(A): \ A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. There exist constants $c,\bar{c}_1,\bar{c}_2$ such that for any $u >0$, conditional on $(P_t)_{t=1}^T$, with probability at least $1-e^{-u}$,
\begin{align*}
&\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta} \right |} -\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr} \left( R\right) \right |}\\
&\le c\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2 \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{u} \sqrt{T}+u\bigg).
\end{align*}
\end{lemma}
The proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:dotprod-fixedP} follows from observing that the entries of $\eta$ are zero mean and $L$-subgaussian, and  applying Theorem~\ref{thm:non-psd-sup} (proof deferred to Appendix~\ref{appsec:proof-lem-dotprod-fixedP}). 

For a suitably large constant $c> 1$, let 
\begin{equation*}
    L_1=c\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right) \bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2 \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)} \sqrt{T}+\log (1/\delta)\bigg),
\end{equation*}
and denote the event
\begin{equation*}
    \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1d}} = \left\{{\forall R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}: \ \ensuremath{\left | \bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta} \right |} \le \sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr} \left( R\right) \right |}+L_1}\right\}.
\end{equation*}
Setting $u=\log(1/\delta)$ in Lemma~\ref{lem:dotprod-fixedP} we can see that
$\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}}\left(\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1d}}\vert (P_t)_{t=1}^T\right) \ge 1-\delta$.

Now, consider $\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr}(R) \right |} = \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr} \left(R_1(A)+R_2(A)+R_3(A)\right) \right |}$. Since $\operatorname{Tr}(R_2(A))=0$ for all $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, we have $\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr}(R) \right |} = \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr} \left(R_1(A)\right)+\operatorname{Tr} \left(R_3(A)\right) \right |}$. For any $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, 
 
 
\begin{align*}
\operatorname{Tr} \left(R_1(A)\right)+\operatorname{Tr} \left(R_3(A)\right) & = \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma\right)\\
&+(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right) \Gamma\right)\\
& = \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma\right)\\
& - \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma\right)\\
&+(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right) \breve{P} \Gamma\right).
\end{align*}
Let $\overline{P}_1 = \breve{P}-pI$ and $\overline{P}_2 = \widetilde{P}-pI$. Then replacing $\breve{P}, \widetilde{P}$ with $\overline{P}_1+pI,\overline{P}_2+pI$ respectively and expanding the terms gives us,
\begin{align*}
\operatorname{Tr} \left(R_1(A)\right)+\operatorname{Tr} \left(R_3(A)\right) & = \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right) - \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right)\\
&+(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\overline{P}_1 \Gamma\right)+p^2(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right)\\
& + p\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \right) \Gamma\right)+2p(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right)\\
& - p\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right)  \right) \Gamma\right).
\end{align*}


Therefore, for any $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, we have
\begin{align*}
\ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr} \left(R_1(A)\right)+\operatorname{Tr} \left(R_3(A)\right) \right |} & \le \bigg |\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right) - \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right)\\
&+(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\overline{P}_1 \Gamma\right)+p^2(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right) \bigg | \\
& + \bigg| p\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \right) \Gamma\right)+2p(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right)\\
& - p\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right)  \right) \Gamma\right)\bigg|.
\end{align*}

Let us define 
\begin{align*}
f_1(A) &= \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right) - \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right)\\
&+(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\overline{P}_1 \Gamma\right).
\end{align*}
Then, we can see that for any  $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$,
\begin{align*}
\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} f_1(A) &= \ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right) - \ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right)\\
&+(1-p)\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\overline{P}_1 \Gamma\right)\\
&=-p(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right)+p(1-p)^2\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right)\\
&=-p^2(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right).
\end{align*}
which implies,
\begin{align*}
\ensuremath{\left | f_1(A)-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} f_1(A) \right |} &= \bigg |\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right) - \operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right)\\
&+(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\overline{P}_1 \Gamma\right)+p^2(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right) \bigg |. 
\end{align*}

Recall the definitions of $q$ and $G$ from the proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:LHS-bound}. We can see that, following the parameterizations in the proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:LHS-bound} to show that,
\begin{align*}
\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right) &= \Big\langle\Gamma \Gamma^\top,\overline{P}_1\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right)\overline{P}_1\Big\rangle\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleG_t,(\overline{P}_1)_t {A^*}^\top A (\overline{P}_1)_t\Big\rangle\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} q_t^\top \left(G_t \odot {A^*}^\top A\right)q_t \\
& = q^\top \left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right)\right) q,
\end{align*}
and,
 
\begin{align*}
\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\overline{P}_1 \Gamma\right) &= \Big\langle\Gamma \Gamma^\top,\overline{P}_1\left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\overline{P}_1\Big\rangle\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleG_t,(\overline{P}_1)_t \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right) (\overline{P}_1)_t\Big\rangle\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} q_t^\top \left(G_t \odot \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)q_t \\
& = q^\top \left(G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)\right) q.
\end{align*}
Let $\widetilde{q} = \operatorname{vec}\left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} q_2 & q_3 & \dots & q_T \end{bmatrix}}\right)$. Then, we can also see that, 
\begin{align*}
\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \overline{P}_1 \right) \Gamma\right) &= \Big\langle(I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}) \Gamma \Gamma^\top,\overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \overline{P}_1\Big\rangle\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \Big\langleA^* G_t,(\overline{P}_2)_t A (\overline{P}_1)_t\Big\rangle\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} q_{t+1}^\top \left((A^*G_t) \odot A\right)q_t \\
& = \widetilde{q}^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes A^*)G\right) \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right)\right) q.
\end{align*}

Now let $\bar{q} = \operatorname{vec}\left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} q_1 & q_2 & q_3 & \dots & q_T \end{bmatrix}}\right)$, and,
\begin{align*}
&S_1(A) = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0_{n \times n(T-1)} & 0_{n \times n} \\ \left((I_{T-1} \otimes A^*)G\right) \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes A\right) & 0_{n(T-1) \times n}  \end{bmatrix}},\\
&S_2(A) = (1-p)\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right) & 0_{n(T-1) \times n} \\ 0_{n \times n(T-1)} & 0_{n \times n} \end{bmatrix}},\\
&S_3(A) = -\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} G \odot \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right) & 0_{n(T-1) \times n} \\ 0_{n \times n(T-1)} & 0_{n \times n} \end{bmatrix}}.
\end{align*}
Thus, for any $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, $f_1(A) = \bar{q}^\top \left(S_1(A)+S_2(A)+S_3(A)\right)\bar{q}$. We can show the following Lemma.

\begin{lemma}\label{lem:trace-uniform-P}
Let $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}} = \left\{{S_1(A)+S_2(A)+S_3(A): A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. There exist constants $\bar{c}_1,\bar{c}_2$ such that for any $u >0$, with probability at least $1-e^{-u}$,
 
 
\begin{equation*}
\sup_{S \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q}-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q} \right |} \le \left((3-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\right) \left(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+u+\sqrt{u}\sqrt{T}\right).
\end{equation*}
\end{lemma}
The proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:trace-uniform-P} also follows from observing the entries of $\bar{q}$ are zero mean and 1-subgaussian, and applying Theorem~\ref{thm:non-psd-sup} (proof deferred to Appendix~\ref{appsec:proof-lem-trace-uniform-P}). Setting $u = \log(1/\delta)$ and denoting
 
\begin{align*}
L_2&=\left((3-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\right) \bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\log (1/\delta)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\sqrt{T}\bigg), \\
 
\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2d}} &= \left\{{\forall S \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}: \ \ensuremath{\left | \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q}-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q} \right |}\le L_2}\right\}.
\end{align*}
 
 
we see that Lemma~\ref{lem:trace-uniform-P} implies $\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}}\left(\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2d}}\right) \ge 1-\delta$.

Now consider 
\begin{align*}
f_2(A) &= \bigg | p\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \overline{P}_2 (I_{T-1} \otimes A) \right) \Gamma\right)+2p(1-p)\operatorname{Tr} \left(\Gamma^\top \overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(A^\top A^*\right)\right)  \Gamma\right)\\
&- p\operatorname{Tr} \left( \Gamma^\top \left(\overline{P}_1 \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \left({A^*}^\top A\right)\right)  \right) \Gamma\right) \bigg |.
\end{align*}
We can bound $f_2(A)$ uniformly over $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$ as follows.
 
 
 
\begin{lemma}\label{lem:dotprod-unifP}
For any $\delta \in (0,1)$, with probability at least $1-\delta$, for any $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$,
\begin{align*}
f_2(A) \le \ \widetilde{c}p(2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2 \sqrt{T} \left( \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right).
\end{align*}
for some constant $\widetilde{c} > 1$.
\end{lemma}
 
 
The proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:dotprod-unifP} follows from an application of Theorem~\ref{thm:bounded-func-conc}. A detailed proof is available in Appendix~\ref{appsec:proof-lem-dotprod-unifP}.


Denoting
\[
\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{3d}} = \left\{{\forall A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}: \ f_2(A) \le \widetilde{c}p(2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2 \sqrt{T} \left( \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right)}\right\},
\]
 we can see that from Lemma~\ref{lem:dotprod-unifP}, $\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}} \left(\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{3d}}\right) \ge 1-\delta$.

On the event $\ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{1d}} \cap \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{2d}} \cap \ensuremath{\ensuremath{\mathcal{E}}_{3d}}$, we have for any $R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}$,
\begin{align*}
\bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta} &\le c(2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2 \bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2 \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)} \sqrt{T}+\log (1/\delta)\bigg)\\
&+cJ(A^*) \bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2 \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)} \sqrt{T}+\log (1/\delta)\bigg)\\
&+\left((3-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\right) \bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\log (1/\delta)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\sqrt{T}\bigg)\\
&+\widetilde{c}2p(2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2 \sqrt{T} \left( \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right)\\
&\le \widetilde{c}\left(J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)  \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)} \sqrt{T}+\log (1/\delta)\right).
\end{align*}
 
This implies,
 
\begin{align}
& \Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,AZ\Big\rangle+(1-p)\Big\langleA^* \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),A\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle \notag \\
& \le c_1\left(J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right) \bigg(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{T\log (1/\delta)}+\log (1/\delta)\bigg) \notag \\
& \le \widetilde{c}_1J^3(A^*) \bigg(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{T\log (1/\delta)}+\log (1/\delta)\bigg). \label{eq:dotprod-PGD-unif-bound}
\end{align}
for some constant $\widetilde{c}_1$ (where the last inequality uses the fact that $J(A^*)\ge 1+\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2$).

Therefore with probability at least $1-\delta$,
\begin{align*}
&2\Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,(\widehat{A}-B)Z\Big\rangle +2(1-p)\Big\langleA^* \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle\\
&+2(1-p)\big\|{(A^*-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F \big\|{(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)}\big\|_F+2\big\|{\left(A^*-B\right)Z}\big\|_F\big\|{(\widehat{A}-B)Z}\big\|_F \\
& \le  c_1 J^3(A^*) \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \bigg(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{T\log \left(1/\delta\right)}+\log (1/\delta)\bigg)\\
&+c_2J^2(A^*) \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\big\|{A^*-B}\big\|_F \bigg(p T+\bar{L}_1 +p(1-p)^2 \bar{L}_1+p\sqrt{T} \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} \bigg(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\bigg)\bigg),
\end{align*}
where 
\begin{align*}
\bar{L}_1 &= C \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}^2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) +\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \sqrt{T}+\sqrt{T} \log \left(1/\delta\right)\right),
\end{align*}
and $C,c_1,c_2$ are constants.
<!-- end proof-21 -->
<!-- claim-8: body_md -->
\label{clm:quad-dotprod-direct} Let $\bar{\eta} = \operatorname{vec}(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \eta_1 & \eta_2 & \dots & \eta_T \end{bmatrix}})$, and 
\[
\breve{P} = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} P_1 & 0 & \dots & 0\\ 0 & P_2 & \dots & 0\\\vdots & \vdots & \dots & \vdots\\ 0 & 0 & \dots & P_{T-1} \end{bmatrix}}, 
 
 
\widetilde{P} = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} P_2 & 0 & \dots & 0\\ 0 & P_3 & \dots & 0\\\vdots & \vdots & \dots & \vdots\\ 0 & 0 & \dots & P_{T} \end{bmatrix}}, \text{ and } \bar{A} = \frac{\widehat{A}-B}{\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F}.
\] Then, it holds that
 
\begin{align*}
\Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)Z\Big\rangle & \\
= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\bar{\eta}^\top \bigg(&   \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma & 0\\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}}\\
&+\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0 & 0\\
\widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \end{bmatrix}}\bigg) \bar{\eta}.
\end{align*}
<!-- end claim-8 -->
<!-- claim-9: body_md -->
\label{clm:quad-dotprod-diag}
Let 
\begin{equation*}
    \breve{P} = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} P_1 & 0 & \dots & 0\\ 0 & P_2 & \dots & 0\\\vdots & \vdots & \dots & \vdots\\ 0 & 0 & \dots & P_{T-1} \end{bmatrix}}, \quad \text{and } \bar{A} = \frac{\widehat{A}-B}{\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F}.
\end{equation*}
Then, it holds that
 
\begin{equation*}
\Big\langleA^* \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F 
\bar{\eta}^\top \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left(\bar{A}^\top A^*\right)\right) \Gamma & 0 \\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}} \bar{\eta},
\end{equation*}
<!-- end claim-9 -->
<!-- lemma-12: body_md -->
\label{lem:dotprod-fixedP}
Let $\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}} = \left\{{R_1(A)+R_2(A)+R_3(A): \ A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. There exist constants $c,\bar{c}_1,\bar{c}_2$ such that for any $u >0$, conditional on $(P_t)_{t=1}^T$, with probability at least $1-e^{-u}$,
\begin{align*}
&\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta} \right |} -\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr} \left( R\right) \right |}\\
&\le c\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2 \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{u} \sqrt{T}+u\bigg).
\end{align*}
<!-- end lemma-12 -->
<!-- lemma-13: body_md -->
\label{lem:trace-uniform-P}
Let $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}} = \left\{{S_1(A)+S_2(A)+S_3(A): A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. There exist constants $\bar{c}_1,\bar{c}_2$ such that for any $u >0$, with probability at least $1-e^{-u}$,
 
 
\begin{equation*}
\sup_{S \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q}-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q} \right |} \le \left((3-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\right) \left(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}} (\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}, \big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+u+\sqrt{u}\sqrt{T}\right).
\end{equation*}
<!-- end lemma-13 -->
<!-- lemma-14: body_md -->
\label{lem:dotprod-unifP}
For any $\delta \in (0,1)$, with probability at least $1-\delta$, for any $A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$,
\begin{align*}
f_2(A) \le \ \widetilde{c}p(2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2 \sqrt{T} \left( \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)+\sqrt{\log (1/\delta)}\right).
\end{align*}
for some constant $\widetilde{c} > 1$.
<!-- end lemma-14 -->
<!-- proof-22: body_md -->
Since $x_{t+1}=A^*x_t+\eta_{t+1}$, and, $y_t=P_t x_t$, we can see that, 
 
\begin{align*}
\left(\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z\right)_{t}
&=P_{t+1}y_{t+1}-A^*P_{t} y_t \\&= P_{t+1} x_{t+1}- P_{t} x_{t}\\ 
&= \left(P_{t+1}A^*-A^*P_t\right)x_t+P_{t+1} \eta_{t+1}.
\end{align*}

Let $R=\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \left(P_{2}A^*-A^*P_1\right)x_1 & \left(P_{3}A^*-A^*P_2\right)x_2&\dots&\left(P_{T}A^*-A^*P_{T-1}\right)x_{T-1} \end{bmatrix}}$ and $E_{proj} = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix}  P_2 \eta_2 & P_3 \eta_3 & \dots & P_T \eta_T \end{bmatrix}}$. Then we can see that,
$\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z=R+E_{proj}$. 

Note that $Z = \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix}  P_1 x_1 & P_2 x_2 & \dots & P_{T-1}x_{T-1} \end{bmatrix}}$. Therefore,
 
\begin{align*}
&\Big\langle\widetilde{Z}-A^*Z,\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)Z\Big\rangle \\
&= \Big\langleR,\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)Z\Big\rangle+\Big\langleE_{proj},\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)Z\Big\rangle\\
& =\operatorname{Tr} \left(\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)ZR^\top\right)+\operatorname{Tr} \left(\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)ZE_{proj}^\top\right)\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \operatorname{Tr}\left(\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t x_t^\top ({A^*}^\top P_{t+1}-P_t {A^*}^\top)\right)+\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \operatorname{Tr}\left(\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t \eta_{t+1}^\top P_{t+1}\right)\\
&=\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} x_t^\top ({A^*}^\top P_{t+1}-P_t {A^*}^\top)\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t +\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \eta_{t+1}^\top P_{t+1}\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t.
\end{align*}

We can use the definitions of the $x,\eta,\Gamma,\widetilde{P}$, and $\breve{P}$, to show that for $\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} x_t^\top ({A^*}^\top P_{t+1}-P_t {A^*}^\top)\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t$,
 
\begin{align*}
\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} x_t^\top ({A^*}^\top P_{t+1}-P_t {A^*}^\top)\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t &= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \sum_{t=1}^{T-1} x_t^\top ({A^*}^\top P_{t+1}-P_t {A^*}^\top)\bar{A} P_t x_t
\end{align*}
Note that,
\begin{align*}
&\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} x_t^\top ({A^*}^\top P_{t+1}-P_t {A^*}^\top)A P_t x_t\\
&= x^\top \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 
({A^*}^\top P_{2}-P_1 {A^*}^\top)\bar{A} P_1 & 0 & \dots & 0\\
0 & ({A^*}^\top P_{3}-P_2 {A^*}^\top)\bar{A} P_2 & \dots & 0 \\
\vdots & \vdots & \dots & \vdots\\
0 & 0 & \dots & ({A^*}^\top P_{T}-P_{T-1} {A^*}^\top)A P_{T-1} \end{bmatrix}} x \\
&=x^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \right) x\\
&=\eta^\top \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma \eta
\end{align*}
and therefore,
\begin{align*}
&\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} x_t^\top ({A^*}^\top P_{t+1}-P_t {A^*}^\top)\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t \\
&= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \eta^\top \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma \eta\\
&=\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\bar{\eta}^\top \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma & 0\\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}}\bar{\eta}
\end{align*}
Let $\widetilde{\eta} = \operatorname{vec}\left(\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \eta_2 & \eta_3 & \dots & \eta_T \end{bmatrix}}\right)$. Then,
\begin{align*}
\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \eta_{t+1}^\top P_{t+1}\left(\widehat{A}-B\right)P_t x_t &= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \eta_{t+1}^\top P_{t+1}\bar{A} P_t x_t= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \widetilde{\eta}^\top \widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} x\\
&= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \widetilde{\eta}^\top \widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \Gamma \eta= \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \bar{\eta}^\top \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0 & 0\\
\widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes \bar{A}) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \end{bmatrix}} \bar{\eta}.
\end{align*}
<!-- end proof-22 -->
<!-- proof-23: body_md -->
Let $\bar{A}=\frac{\widehat{A}-B}{\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F}$. Note that,
 
\begin{align*}
\Big\langleA^*  \operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top),(\widehat{A}-B)\operatorname{diag}^{1/2}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle
&=\operatorname{Tr} \left((\widehat{A}-B)^\top A^*  \operatorname{diag}(ZZ^\top)\right)\\
&=\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|\operatorname{Tr} \left(A^\top A^*  \operatorname{diag}(ZZ^\top)\right)\\
&=\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|\Big\langle{A^*}^\top \bar{A},\operatorname{diag}(ZZ^\top)\Big\rangle\\ 
& = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\Big\langle\operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right),\operatorname{diag}\left(ZZ^\top\right)\Big\rangle\\
& = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\sum_{i=1}^n \left(\operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right)\right)_{ii} \left({\operatorname{diag}\left(ZZ^\top\right)}\right)_{ii}\\
& = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\sum_{i=1}^n \left(\operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right)\right)_{ii} \sum_{t=1}^{T-1} Z_{it}^2\\
& = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \sum_{i=1}^n \left(\operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right)\right)_{ii}  Z_{it}^2\\
& = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F\sum_{t=1}^{T-1} \left(Z_{:,t}\right)^\top \operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right) Z_{:,t}\\
& = \big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \operatorname{vec}{(Z)}^\top \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right)\right) \operatorname{vec}{(Z)}\\
&=\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F \eta^\top \Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right)\right) \breve{P} \Gamma \eta\\
&=\big\|{\widehat{A}-B}\big\|_F  \bar{\eta}^\top \ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} \Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left({A^*}^\top \bar{A}\right)\right) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \\ 0 & 0 \end{bmatrix}} \bar{\eta}.
\end{align*}
<!-- end proof-23 -->
<!-- proof-24: body_md -->
Since $\bar{\eta}$ is zero mean and $L$-subgaussian, we can see that, $\eta_{L} = \frac{\bar{\eta}}{L}$ is 1-subgaussian. Therefore, applying Theorem~\ref{thm:non-psd-sup} on $\eta_{L}^\top R \eta_{L}$ we get that,
\begin{align*}
\ensuremath{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \eta_{L}^\top R \eta_{L} - \ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \left[\eta_{L}^\top R \eta_{L} \bigg \vert (P_t)_{t=1}^T \right] \right |} \ge c' \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\right)+\sqrt{u} d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}})+ud_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}) \ \bigg \vert (P_t)_{t=1}^T \bigg) \le e^{-u}.
\end{align*}
Note that since each entry of $\bar{\eta}$ has unit variance, $\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \left[\eta_{L}^\top R \eta_{L} \bigg \vert (P_t)_{t=1}^T \right] = \frac{1}{L^2}\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \left[\bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta}\right] = \frac{1}{L^2}\operatorname{Tr}(R)$. Therefore, conditional on $(P_t)_{t=1}^T$, with probability at least $1-e^{-u}$,
\begin{align*}
\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \eta_{L}^\top R \eta_{L} - \frac{1}{L^2}\operatorname{Tr} \left( R\right) \right |} \le c' \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\right)+\sqrt{u} d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}})+ud_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}).
\end{align*}
 
 
which implies
 
\begin{align*}
\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta}-\operatorname{Tr} \left( R\right) \right |} \le c\left(c' \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\right)+\sqrt{u} d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}})+ud_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}})\right).
\end{align*}
where $c$ is a constant.

Similar to the proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:M1-expec} we next show that the maps $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)$ and $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)$ are Lipschitz. 

\begin{claim}\label{clm:lip-map-calR}
The following statements hold a.s.
 
\begin{enumerate}
\item The map $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)$ is $\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)$-Lipschitz, and,
\item The map $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)$ is $\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\sqrt{T}$-Lipschitz.
\end{enumerate}
\end{claim}

Hence from Property~\ref{itm:prop-3} from Appendix~\ref{sec:prelim-tools}, we get,
\begin{align*}
&\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right) \le c_1 \left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right) \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2\right),\\
&\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right) \le c_1 \left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\sqrt{T} \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}\left(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F\right).
\end{align*}
We can also see that,
\begin{align*}
d_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}) &= \sup_{R\in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \big\|{R}\big\|_2 = \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{R_1(A)+R_2(A)+R_3(A)}\big\|_2\\ 
&\le\sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right) \big\|{A}\big\|_2  \quad \text{a.s} \\
& \le \left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right), \;\text{and},\\
d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}) &= \sup_{R\in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \big\|{R}\big\|_F = \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}} \big\|{R_1(A)+R_2(A)+R_3(A)}\big\|_F\\
& \le \sup_{A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\sqrt{T} \big\|{A}\big\|_F \quad \text{a.s}\\
& = \left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\sqrt{T}.
\end{align*}

Therefore, conditional on $(P_t)_{t=1}^T$, with probability at least $1-e^{-u}$,
\begin{align*}
&\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta} \right |} -\sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \operatorname{Tr} \left( R\right) \right |} \le \sup_{R \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{\eta}^\top R \bar{\eta}-\operatorname{Tr} \left( R\right) \right |}\\
&\le c\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right) \bigg(\bar{c}_1 \ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\bar{c}_2 \ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{u} \sqrt{T}+u\bigg).
\end{align*}
<!-- end proof-24 -->
<!-- claim-10: body_md -->
\label{clm:lip-map-calR}
The following statements hold a.s.
 
\begin{enumerate}
\item The map $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)$ is $\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)$-Lipschitz, and,
\item The map $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)$ is $\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\sqrt{T}$-Lipschitz.
\end{enumerate}
<!-- end claim-10 -->
<!-- proof-25: body_md -->
Let $A_1,A_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}$, and $\widetilde{R}_1, \widetilde{R}_2 \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{R}}$ be such that $\widetilde{R}_1 = R_1(A_1)+R_2(A_1)+R_3(A_1)$ and $\widetilde{R}_2 = R_1(A_2)+R_2(A_2)+R_3(A_2)$. Then,
\begin{align*}
\big\|{\widetilde{R}_1-\widetilde{R}_2}\big\|_2 & = \big\|{R_1(A_1)+R_2(A_1)+R_3(A_1)-R_1(A_2)-R_2(A_2)-R_3(A_2)}\big\|_2\\
& \le \big\|{R_1(A_1)-R_1(A_2)}\big\|_2+\big\|{R_2(A_1)-R_2(A_2)}\big\|_2+\big\|{R_3(A_1)-R_3(A_2)}\big\|_2\\
& = \big\|{\Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes (A_1-A_2)) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma}\big\|_2 \\
&+\big\|{\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0 & 0\\
\widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes (A_1-A_2)) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \end{bmatrix}}}\big\|_2+\big\|{(1-p)\Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left((A_1-A_2)^\top A^*\right)\right) \Gamma}\big\|_2\\
& \le \big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2^2\big\|{\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right)}\big\|_2 \big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2 \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2 \\
&+\big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2 \big\|{\widetilde{P}}\big\|_2 \big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2 \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2+(1-p)\big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2^2\big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2\big\|{ \operatorname{diag}\left((A_1-A_2)^\top A^*\right)}\big\|_2\\
& = \big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2^2\big\|{\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right)}\big\|_2 \big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2 \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2 \\
&+\big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2 \big\|{\widetilde{P}}\big\|_2 \big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2 \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2+(1-p)\big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2^2\big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2\max_{i \in [n]} {\left((A_1-A_2)_{:,i}\right)}^\top {(A^{*})}_{:,i}\\
& \le J^2(A^*) \big\|{A^*}\big\|_2 \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2 +J(A^*) \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2+(1-p)J^2(A^*) \big\|{\left((A_1-A_2)\right)}\big\|_2 \big\|{{(A^{*})}}\big\|_2 \quad \text{a.s}\\
&=\left((2-p)J^2(A^*) \big\|{\left((A_1-A_2)\right)}\big\|_2+J(A^*) \right) \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_2 
\end{align*}
where $(A_1-A_2)_{:,i}$ and ${(A^{*})}_{:,i}$ are the $i$-th columns of $A_1-A_2$ and $A^*$ respectively. Therefore, the map $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)$ is $\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)$-Lipschitz a.s.

To show that  $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)$ is Lipschitz, we can follow the same arguments as above.
\begin{align*}
\big\|{\widetilde{R}_1-\widetilde{R}_2}\big\|_F & = \big\|{R_1(A_1)+R_2(A_1)+R_3(A_1)-R_1(A_2)-R_2(A_2)-R_3(A_2)}\big\|_F\\
& \le \big\|{R_1(A_1)-R_1(A_2)}\big\|_F+\big\|{R_2(A_1)-R_2(A_2)}\big\|_F+\big\|{R_3(A_1)-R_3(A_2)}\big\|_F\\
& = \big\|{\Gamma^\top \left(\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right) (I_{T-1} \otimes (A_1-A_2)) \breve{P} \right) \Gamma}\big\|_F \\
&+\big\|{\ensuremath{\begin{bmatrix} 0 & 0\\
\widetilde{P}(I_{T-1} \otimes (A_1-A_2)) \breve{P} \Gamma & 0 \end{bmatrix}}}\big\|_F+\big\|{(1-p)\Gamma^\top \breve{P} \left(I_{T-1} \otimes \operatorname{diag}\left((A_1-A_2)^\top A^*\right)\right) \Gamma}\big\|_F\\
& \le \big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2^2\big\|{\left((I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top) \widetilde{P}-\breve{P} (I_{T-1} \otimes {A^*}^\top)\right)}\big\|_2 \big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2 \sqrt{T}\big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F \\
&+\big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2 \big\|{\widetilde{P}}\big\|_2 \big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2 \sqrt{T}\big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F+(1-p)\big\|{\Gamma}\big\|_2^2\big\|{\breve{P}}\big\|_2\sqrt{T}\big\|{ \operatorname{diag}\left((A_1-A_2)^\top A^*\right)}\big\|_F\\
& \le J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\sqrt{T} \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F +J(A^*) \sqrt{T}\big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F\\
&+(1-p)J^2(A^*)\sqrt{T} \left(\sum_{i \in [n]} \left({\left((A_1-A_2)_{:,i}\right)}^\top {(A^{*})}_{:,i}\right)^2\right)^{1/2}\\
& \le J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\sqrt{T} \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F +J(A^*) \sqrt{T}\big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F\\
&+(1-p)J^2(A^*)\sqrt{T} \left(\sum_{i \in [n]}\big\|{\left((A_1-A_2)_{:,i}\right)}\big\|_2^2 \big\|{{(A^{*})}_{:,i}}\big\|_2^2\right)^{1/2} \quad \text{a.s}\\
& \le J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\sqrt{T} \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F +J(A^*) \sqrt{T}\big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F\\
&+(1-p)J^2(A^*)\sqrt{T} \big\|{A^*}\big\|_2\left(\sum_{i \in [n]}\big\|{\left((A_1-A_2)_{:,i}\right)}\big\|_2^2\right)^{1/2}\\
& \le \bigg( J^2(A^*) \big\|{A^*}\big\|_2 +J(A^*) +(1-p)J^2(A^*)  \big\|{{(A^{*})}}\big\|_2 \bigg) \sqrt{T} \big\|{\left((A_1-A_2)\right)}\big\|_F \\
&=\left((2-p)J^2(A^*) \big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*) \right)\sqrt{T} \big\|{A_1-A_2}\big\|_F.
\end{align*}
Therefore, the map $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{R}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)$ is $\left((2-p)J^2(A^*)\big\|{A^*}\big\|_2+J(A^*)\right)\sqrt{T}$-Lipschitz a.s.
<!-- end proof-25 -->
<!-- proof-26: body_md -->
Let $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}} = \left\{{S_1(A)+S_2(A)+S_3(A): A \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B}}\right\}$. Since each $\bar{q}_i \in [-p,1-p]$ for $i \in [nT]$ and centered, we can see that, entries of $\bar{q}$ are zero mean, 1-subgaussian. Therefore, we can apply Theorem~\ref{thm:non-psd-sup} on $\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}$ to show that for any $u>0$, with probability $1-e^{-u}$,
\begin{align*}
\sup_{S \in \ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}} \ensuremath{\left | \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q}-\ensuremath{\mathbb{E}} \bar{q}^\top S \bar{q} \right |} \le c' \left(\ensuremath{\gamma_{1}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)+\ensuremath{\gamma_{2}}(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)\right)+ud_2(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}})+\sqrt{u}d_F(\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}}).
\end{align*}
Similar to in the proof of Lemma~\ref{lem:M1-expec} we next show that $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_2)$ and $(\ensuremath{\mathcal{C}}_{B},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F) \mapsto (\ensuremath{\mathcal{S}},\big\|{\cdot}\big\|_F)$ are Lipschitz.
